2026-10-10T04:09:25.0031103Z   QWEN_COMMIT_MESSAGE: [cluster:aspire2ap] [nodes:2] Page KV in 64-token blocks with FA3 on P3/D1 c72

Why: the one-GPU attention probe 0e65dee (Actions 38022625925, PBS218312,
H100, decode-verify shapes: 16 Q heads, 1 FP8 KV head, 4 verify tokens,
4096-token prefix, scattered pages) measured FA3 exactly as SGLang calls it
in target verify:
- page size 1:  batch 64 51.3 us, batch 96 80.7 us per layer call
- page size 64: batch 64 43.0 us, batch 96 65.8 us (-16% / -19%)
with identical error against an FP32 reference (rel L2 ~0.027 from FP8 Q).
The same probe found FlashInfer XQA numerically correct in isolation
(rel L2 0.0267 with SGLang's prefix+window seq_lens and HND view) but only
0-9% faster than FA3 at page 64, so the broken serving result of 662561f
came from the backend integration, and the kernel swap is not worth
pursuing. Page size is the cheap part of that gain.

Change (runtime only, existing image lineage): configs/aspire-serving.json
page_size 64 passed as --page-size to every ASPIRE worker (PD transfer
requires equal page sizes; chunk 8192 is divisible). FA3 remains the
attention backend everywhere. Serving is otherwise ed8c534: P3/D1, decode
pool 393216, c72, Triton decode MoE, EAGLE3 steps3/topk1/draft4, FP8 KV.

Expected: decode attention ~1 ms less per verify step at ~60 running
(~3% ITL), plus fewer, larger NIXL transfer descriptors. Compare with
ed8c534 (3313.0 good tok/s, ITL 17.18 ms at 61.6 running); different node
pair likely, so not a matched pair.

Budget: myprojects 59010014 balance 13529.208 SU, In Doubt 0 before this
push (the <=32 SU probe may not be settled yet). This job reserves at most
1024 SU.

Validation: bash -n, Ruff and the full CPU pytest suite with --page-size 64
in every ASPIRE worker argv.
